========== ABASE/PMBASE =========

PMBASE = 0x1800 (IO)

pmbase+0x0000: 0x0000     (PM1_STS)
pmbase+0x0002: 0x0120     (PM1_EN)
pmbase+0x0004: 0x00000001 (PM1_CNT)
pmbase+0x0008: 0x0059f3d0 (PM1_TMR)
pmbase+0x0030: 0x80002033 (SMI_EN)
pmbase+0x0034: 0x00004000 (SMI_STS)
pmbase+0x0040: 0x00000000 (GPE_CNTL)
pmbase+0x0044: 0x1060     (DEVACT_STS)
pmbase+0x0050: 0x00000000 (PM2_CNT)
pmbase+0x0054: 0x00002000 (OC_WDT_CTL)
pmbase+0x0080: 0x00000000 (GPE0_STS_31_0)
pmbase+0x0084: 0x00000000 (GPE0_STS_63_31)
pmbase+0x0088: 0x00000000 (GPE0_STS_95_64)
pmbase+0x008c: 0x00000000 (GPE0_STS_127_96)

pmbase+0x009c: 0x00002246 (GPE0_EN_127_96)

============= AMBs ============

============= SPI / BIOS CNTL =============

BIOS_CNTL = 0x0080 (IO)

0x0000 = BIOSWE - write enable
0x0000 = BLE - lock enable
0x0000 = SPI Read configuration
0x0000 = TopSwapStatus
0x0000 = SMM BIOS Write Protect Disable
0x0002 = reserved

============= SPI Bar ==============

0x36fb6b34 = BFPR - BIOS Flash primary region
0x0000ff99 = HSFSTS - Hardware Sequencing Flash Status
0x00002dbd = HSFCTL - Hardware Sequencing Flash Control
0x39fa693d = FADDR - Flash Address
0x2da3ef8a = Reserved
0x21f32b35 = FDATA0
0xc501bc9f = FRACC - Flash Region Access Permissions
0x11e495c7 = Flash Region 0
0x8d013c8c = Flash Region 1
0x19e495c7 = Flash Region 2
0xc503b48c = Flash Region 3
0x15a4bcc7 = Flash Region 4
0xb13a3e85 = FPR0 Flash Protected Range 0
0x05972606 = FPR0 Flash Protected Range 1
0x51261b4f = FPR0 Flash Protected Range 2

0x0000895f = OPTYPE - Opcode Type Configuration
0x8857b9db4a631cf2 = OPMENU - Opcode Menu Configuration
0x000000f2 = BBAR - BIOS Base Address Configuration
0x6be98da8 = FDOC - Flash Descriptor Observability Control
0xcbe18c11 = Reserved

0xafc9e4c3 = LVSCC - Host Lower Vendor Specific Component Capabilities
0xb97a3399 = UVSCC - Host Upper Vendor Specific Component Capabilities
0x3bfab799 = FPB - Flash Partition Boundary
